  Availability and implementation: COMPASS is implemented in Python and is freely available at https://github.com/ rsinghlab/COMPASS. Installation instructions, pretrained weights, and example usage are provided in the repository.
  Contact: jack_roberts2@brown.edu, ritsingh@illinois.edu
  Supplementary information: Available onlime upon publication.

</details>


### [69] [Decouple, Purify and Unite: Semantic-Structural Prototype Learning for Federated Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04700)
*Xingyue Zhao,Wenke Huang,Linghao Zhuang,Yanzhou Su,Zhifeng Wang,Haoyu Zhao,Mengfan Li,Junjun He,Tao Tan,Dakai Jin,Le Lu,Mang Ye,Qiang Yang,Ming Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedBCS+，通过频域风格重校准、解耦上下文原型对齐与风格纯化语义原型聚合，缓解联邦医学分割中的特征异质性，在五个异构基准上取得最高平均Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可在不共享数据的前提下训练全局模型，但不同扫描仪或协议带来的特征异质性仍是挑战。现有基于表征的方法存在两点不足：一是上下文表征学习不完整，单层或耦合表征忽略多层级结构线索，并将区域语义与边界细节纠缠；二是存在逐层风格偏差与聚合偏差，中间层的域特定风格差异会损害原型，而忽略客户端分布偏移的聚合会进一步放大偏差。

Method: 提出FedBCS+（联邦解耦上下文对齐与风格纯化聚合）。包含三个组件：1）频域风格重校准（FSR），在原型构建中解耦内容-风格表征并提取风格纯化原型；2）解耦上下文原型对齐（DCPA），将多层级特征显式解耦为语义原型与结构原型，分别对齐区域语义和细粒度解剖结构；3）风格纯化语义原型聚合（S2PA），度量各客户端纯化原型与全局共识的偏差，自适应地向上代表不足的客户端重新加权聚合，以减少共识偏差。

Result: 在涵盖组织病理学、MRI、超声和结肠镜的五个异构医学分割基准上，FedBCS+在所比较方法中取得最高平均Dice。收敛性分析进一步刻画了聚合与对齐如何影响优化界。

Conclusion: FedBCS+通过风格纯化与解耦对齐有效应对联邦医学图像分割中的特征异质性，性能优于对比方法，并给出了聚合与对齐对优化界影响的理论分析。

Abstract: Federated learning enables medical institutions to train a global model without sharing data, yet feature heterogeneity from diverse scanners or protocols remains challenging. Existing representation-based methods face two limitations: 1) Incomplete Contextual Representation Learning: single-layer or coupled representations overlook multi-level structural cues and entangle regional semantics with boundary details. 2) Layerwise Style and Aggregation Biases: domain-specific style discrepancies across intermediate layers degrade prototypes, while aggregation that overlooks client distribution shifts can further amplify bias. We propose FedBCS+, federated decoupled contextual alignment with style-purified aggregation. We employ Frequency-domain Style Recalibration (FSR) in prototype construction to decouple content-style representations and extract style-purified prototypes. Built upon these purified features, Decoupled Contextual Prototype Alignment (DCPA) explicitly decouples multi-level features into semantic and structural prototypes and aligns regional semantics and fine-grained anatomical structures separately. Style-purified Semantic Prototype Aggregation (S2PA) measures each client's purified prototype divergence from the global consensus and adaptively reweights aggregation toward under-represented clients to reduce consensus bias. On five heterogeneous medical segmentation benchmarks spanning histopathology, MRI, ultrasound, and colonoscopy, FedBCS+ achieves the highest mean Dice among the compared methods. A convergence analysis further characterizes how aggregation and alignment affect the optimization bound.

</details>


### [70] [Knossos and Ariadne: Benchmarking and Learning Complete Diagram Topology Extraction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04721)
*Bangwei Guo,Xujiang Zhao,Shengyu Chen,Yanchi Liu,Wei Cheng,Xi Zhu,Guoning Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究"图到拓扑结构抽取"任务，提出大规模基准Knossos和结构化框架Ariadne，实现完整拓扑的高效抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽能识别图元素并推理内容，但完整图拓扑抽取（所有实体及完整关系）研究不足，缺乏大规模有监督训练与系统评测的资源。

Method: 构建Knossos基准（19,200个图、6个领域、245,179节点、439,740边），通过符号生成保证渲染图与完整拓扑、关系类型、连接线几何的精确对齐；提出Ariadne框架，将任务分解为节点清单抽取与源条件边预测。

Result: 在Knossos上训练显著提升小型开源VLM的完整拓扑抽取能力；Ariadne在同等监督下优于一步式抽取，在Knossos上取得最高平均Edge F1，且两种骨干变体在真实外部基准上均优于未适配版本。

Conclusion: 结构化分解与大规模符号对齐数据可有效推动完整图拓扑抽取，代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: Structural diagrams are widely used to represent complex systems and relational information across scientific, engineering, procedural, and spatial domains. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) have become increasingly capable of recognizing diagram elements and reasoning about their content, while complete diagram topology extraction remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we study diagram-to-graph topology extraction: extracting all diagram entities and the complete relations among them. To enable large-scale supervised training and systematic evaluation of this task, we introduce Knossos, a benchmark of 19,200 diagrams across six diverse domains, with 245,179 nodes and 439,740 edges. Its symbolic generation process provides exact alignment between rendered diagrams and annotations of complete topology, relation types, and connector geometry. To address the modeling challenge of complete topology extraction, we also present Ariadne, a structured framework that decomposes the task into node inventory extraction and source-conditioned edge prediction. Extensive experiments show that training on Knossos substantially improves complete topology extraction in smaller open-source VLMs. Ariadne further improves over one-step extraction under matched supervision, demonstrating the additional benefit of structured decomposition. It achieves the highest average Edge F1 among the evaluated methods on Knossos, while both backbone variants also improve over their unadapted counterparts on the real-world external benchmark. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/bangwayne/knossos_Ariadne_Public.

</details>


### [71] [NAMVIS: Next-Scale Autoregressive Multi-View Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04722)
*Ramil Khafizov,Ilya Statsenko,Ruslan Rakhimov,Artem Komarichev,Peter Wonka,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAMVIS 提出一种免扩散框架，将稀疏视角多视图合成重构为几何条件下的 next-scale 自回归生成，在保持多视图一致性的同时实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角新视角合成是 3D 内容生成的核心问题，但基于扩散的方法受限于迭代去噪过程，导致多视图生成推理成本高昂。因此需要一种更高效的多视图合成替代方案。

Method: NAMVIS 将多视图图像合成重构为几何条件下的 next-scale 自回归：通过少量由粗到细的尺度步骤预测离散视觉 token，同一尺度内及跨目标视图的 token 并行采样。为实现相机几何锚定，提出多尺度投影位姿编码（Multi-scale Projective Pose Encoding），在每个分辨率下将源与目标相机变换注入目标视图自注意力和源到目标交叉注意力；并结合全局条件与密集几何感知交叉注意力。

Result: 在 Objaverse、GSO 和 OmniObject3D 上，NAMVIS 在 PSNR、SSIM 和 LPIPS 指标上均优于扩散基线，且在相同评估设置下推理速度比所评估的扩散基线快 3 倍以上。

Conclusion: 几何条件下的 next-scale 自回归是稀疏视角多视图合成中一种有前景且高效的扩散替代方案。更多定性结果、视频与资源见项目主页。

Abstract: Sparse-view novel view synthesis is a central problem in 3D content creation, but diffusion-based approaches remain limited by iterative denoising, making multi-view generation expensive at inference time. We introduce NAMVIS, a diffusion-free framework that reformulates multi-view image synthesis as geometry-conditioned next-scale autoregression. Instead of generating target views through repeated denoising, NAMVIS predicts discrete visual tokens through a small number of coarse-to-fine scale steps, while sampling all tokens within each scale and across target views in parallel. To anchor this generation process to explicit camera geometry, we propose Multi-scale Projective Pose Encoding, which injects source and target camera transformations into both target-view self-attention and source-to-target cross-attention at every resolution. NAMVIS further combines global conditioning with dense geometry-aware cross-attention, enabling the model to preserve source-view appearance while maintaining target-view consistency. Across Objaverse, GSO, and OmniObject3D, NAMVIS outperforms diffusion-based baselines in PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS, while running over 3 times faster than the evaluated diffusion baselines under the same evaluation setting. These results suggest that geometry-conditioned next-scale autoregression is a promising and efficient alternative to diffusion for sparse-view multi-view synthesis. Additional qualitative results, videos, and resources are available at https://corl-team.github.io/namvis/

</details>


### [72] [Investigating Spatiotemporal Redundancy in Video Transformer for Collision Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04727)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析VideoMAEv2-Base在碰撞预测任务中的冗余结构，提出时间token合并与MLP神经元剪枝两种方法，在几乎不损失性能的前提下将GFLOPs减半、速度提升1.76倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频Transformer在工人-设备接近监测中性能优异，但计算量大，难以满足移动机器人低延迟推理与建筑领域低碳计算的需求，因此需要探究其计算冗余是否可被去除而不显著损害预测性能。

Method: 基于VideoMAEv2-Base与Nexar Collision Prediction数据集，先用线性探针考察碰撞相关信息随网络深度的演变，再研究两类冗余：MLP中的结构化容量冗余与token流中的时空冗余，并据此实施时间token合并与重要性引导的MLP单元保留（剪枝）。

Result: 线性探针显示运动线索（流幅值、临近、接近/远离）在中间层最易获取，碰撞标签判别力在末层增强。token冗余具有轴向特异性：后层相邻时间token相似度达0.970，空间相似度仅0.297。时间token合并使计算量从356.99降至178.50 GFLOPs，延迟从12.69降至7.21 ms，加速1.76倍，mAP仅从0.7478变为0.7443。保留50% MLP单元使AUC保持0.753，而随机保留仅0.529，并发现剪枝先破坏分数校准、后导致判别排序崩溃。

Conclusion: 研究建立了通过针对性时间token压缩与神经元剪枝实现更快算法的新路径。

Abstract: In worker-equipment proximity monitoring, video transformers are widely used for collision anticipation and have demonstrated strong performance. However, their accuracy comes with substantial computational demands, creating a tension with the need for low-latency inference on mobile robots and the pursuit of lower-carbon computation in construction. To address this, this study investigates where computation within an established video transformer is redundant and whether that redundancy can be removed without materially degrading predictive performance. Using VideoMAEv2-Base on the Nexar Collision Prediction dataset, we first examine how collision-relevant information evolves across network depth and then investigate two complementary forms of redundancy: structured capacity redundancy in multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and spatiotemporal redundancy in the token stream. Linear probes show that interpretable motion cues, including flow magnitude, looming, and approach versus retreat, are most accessible at intermediate layers, whereas collision-label discrimination strengthens toward the final layer. Token redundancy is axis-specific: adjacent temporal-token similarity reaches 0.970 in later layers, while spatial similarity falls to 0.297, indicating substantially greater redundancy across time than across space. Exploiting this asymmetry, temporal token merging reduces backbone computation from 356.99 to 178.50 GFLOPs and latency from 12.69 to 7.21 ms per clip, a 1.76x speedup, while mean average precision changes only from 0.7478 to 0.7443. Importance-guided retention of 50% of MLP units preserves an AUC of 0.753, compared with 0.529 under matched random retention, and reveals that pruning alters score calibration before discriminative ranking collapses. These findings establish a new pathway for pursuing faster algorithms through targeted temporal token compression and neuron pruning.

</details>


### [73] [Probabilistic Pedestrian Forecasts from a Handheld Phone: World-Frame Heat Maps, Visual-Inertial Height Drift, and Evaluation without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04736)
*Danial Safaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并评估一套仅用单目相机与VIO、以地面为参照的行人未来位置预测系统，在地面保持、标定与自一致性评估上取得优于匀速基线的结果，但效果对重采样与重标定敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持手机的行人若能被告知附近他人未来几秒的位置，将有实用价值；但这一设想需要满足若干前提：预测须停留在地面参考系中、随手机移动、经过标定，且仅依赖单目相机与视觉惯性里程计（VIO）。论文旨在构建并检验这样一个系统是否可行。

Method: 检测行人后通过射线—平面求交将其投影到地面，在重力对齐的度量坐标系中跟踪，并用小型U-Net以负对数似然损失在鸟瞰（SDD）与第一人称（EgoTraj-Bench）轨迹上训练，输出逐步概率图作为预测。针对手持ADVIO录像的垂直VIO漂移与用户自身楼层变化，采用低通滤波高度并固定相机离地高度来抑制尺度漂移；因手持视频缺乏行人真值，用跟踪器自身后续原始测量作为评分依据，并进行内部预注册评估。

Result: 在ADVIO手持录像上，垂直VIO漂移与楼层变化会静默重缩放单目地面位置（某片段90秒内达87%；另一片段最后31%的轨迹全部丢失），固定相机离地高度可避免该问题但扶梯场景有滞后。在SDD与EgoTraj-Bench测试集上，最终预测器在4.8秒处相比拟合的匀速高斯基线分别降低NLL 1.51与1.37 nats。在七个留出片段的预注册评估中，NLL在1.2、2.4、4.8秒均低于基线（0.17、0.23、0.47 nats，95%区间不含零），但幅度不及开发片段的一半。探索性分析结果双向：按片段重采样会使1.2与2.4秒区间包含零；双方在开发片段重标定后网络仅在1.2秒显著更优；而两个使用ADVIO参考位姿的片段在改用手机自身位姿后更有利于网络。

Conclusion: 系统在受控条件下优于匀速基线，但优势对评估方式（按人还是按片段重采样）、重新标定及位姿来源高度敏感，说明此类自一致性评分能提供有限但非决定性的证据，其可证明与不可证明的边界需要谨慎界定。

Abstract: A pedestrian with a phone could be shown where nearby people will be in the next few seconds, if the forecast stays on the ground while the phone moves, is calibrated, and needs only a monocular camera and visual-inertial odometry (VIO). We build and evaluate such a system. People are detected, lifted onto the floor by ray-plane intersection, tracked in a gravity-aligned metric frame, and forecast as per-step probability maps by a small U-Net trained with a negative log-likelihood (NLL) loss on bird's-eye (SDD) and first-person (EgoTraj-Bench) trajectories. On handheld ADVIO recordings, vertical VIO drift and the user's own changes of level silently rescale monocular ground positions (by 87% within 90 s on one clip; on another, all tracks are lost for the last 31% of the clip); keeping the camera's height above the floor constant under a low-pass-filtered altitude avoids this, though it lags on escalators. On the SDD and EgoTraj-Bench test splits, the final forecaster lowers the NLL at 4.8 s by 1.51 and 1.37 nats relative to a fitted constant-velocity Gaussian. Lacking ground truth for people in handheld video, we score forecasts against the tracker's own later raw measurements. In an internally pre-registered evaluation on seven held-out clips, the final forecaster's NLL is lower than the benchmark-fitted baseline's at 1.2, 2.4 and 4.8 s (by 0.17, 0.23 and 0.47 nats; 95% intervals over people exclude zero), but by less than half as much as on the development clips. Exploratory analyses cut both ways: resampling clips instead of people widens the intervals to include zero at 1.2 and 2.4 s, and once both forecasters are recalibrated on the development clips the network is significantly better only at 1.2 s; but two clips run with ADVIO's reference poses favour the network much more when re-run with the phone's own poses. We discuss what such self-consistency scores can and cannot show.

</details>


### [74] [ThyCLIPNet: A BiomedCLIP-Guided Lightweight Attention-Enhanced DeepLabV3+ Framework for Robust Thyroid Nodule Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04743)
*Tasnim Jahan,Md Easin Arafat,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThyCLIPNet：一种轻量级语义引导混合编解码框架，首次（之一）利用BiomedCLIP视觉编码器提供纯图像全局语义引导，实现高效准确的甲状腺超声分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声分割面临低对比度、斑点噪声和边界模糊的挑战；现有方法依赖资源密集型架构，或缺乏多尺度特征与全局生物医学视觉引导的显式融合。

Method: 编码器：MobileNetV2 + 高效通道注意力，瓶颈特征由空洞空间金字塔池化（ASPP）和自定义卷积块注意力模块增强。解码器：分层跳跃连接 + 轻量注意力细化 + BiomedCLIP引导的门控融合路径，将纯视觉全局生物医学嵌入投影到解码器特征空间，并通过语义-局部融合与空间门控选择性整合。

Result: 在TG3K、TN3K、DDTI、PKTN上Dice分别为96.22%、87.58%、84.73%、80.70%；IoU为92.72%、77.91%、73.51%、67.64%；95% Hausdorff距离为3.75、16.38、18.23、10.86。参数量8.55M，FLOPs 22.99G。

Conclusion: 结果支持将全局生物医学语义引导与轻量多尺度CNN表示相结合，可实现鲁棒且计算高效的甲状腺超声分割。

Abstract: Accurate thyroid ultrasound segmentation is often challenged by low contrast, speckle noise, and unclear boundaries. Although recent methods have improved segmentation accuracy, many rely on resource-intensive architectures or lack explicit integration of multiscale features with global biomedical visual guidance. In this paper, we introduce ThyCLIPNet, a lightweight semantic-guided hybrid encoder-decoder framework that integrates BiomedCLIP-derived biomedical semantic guidance into a lightweight multi-scale CNN segmentation pipeline. The encoder integrates MobileNetV2 with efficient channel attention, while atrous spatial pyramid pooling and a custom convolutional block attention module enrich bottleneck features. The decoder combines hierarchical skip connections and lightweight attention refinement with a BiomedCLIP-guided gated fusion pathway that projects vision-only global biomedical embeddings into decoder feature space and selectively integrates them through semantic-local fusion and spatial gating. To the best of our knowledge, ThyCLIPNet is among the first lightweight thyroid ultrasound segmentation frameworks to use BiomedCLIP's vision encoder alone for image-only global semantic guidance without text prompting. Experiments on TG3K, TN3K, DDTI, and PKTN achieve dice similarity coefficients of 96.22%, 87.58%, 84.73%, and 80.70%; intersection over union scores of 92.72%, 77.91%, 73.51%, and 67.64%; and 95th-percentile hausdorff distances of 3.75, 16.38, 18.23, and 10.86, respectively. ThyCLIPNet uses 8.55M parameters and 22.99G FLOPs. Overall, the results support integrating global biomedical semantic guidance with lightweight multi-scale CNN representations for robust and computationally efficient thyroid ultrasound segmentation. Source code: https://github.com/Tasnim-Jahan/ThyCLIPNet. [Abstract shortened for arXiv. See PDF for full abstract.]

</details>


### [75] [ARISE: Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounded Self-Evaluation for Interpretable IBD Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04777)
*Pronoma Banerjee,Anuva Shah,Jason Wu,Md. Masudur Rahman,Sanjay Mohanty,Satya Kurada,Juan P. Wachs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARISE 是一种自主规划框架，将少样本医学图像理解建模为序贯智能体工作流，用于炎症性肠病（IBD）的多模态影像诊断，在提升诊断性能的同时增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBD 需要频繁影像评估，但无线胶囊内镜（WCE）和肠道超声等模态的判读高度依赖专家经验；视觉语言模型（VLM）虽具潜力，却存在领域推理不足、易产生幻觉、细粒度诊断训练数据稀缺以及可解释性有限等问题，阻碍临床落地。

Method: 提出 ARISE（基于图像锚定自评估的自适应智能体推理），将少样本医学图像理解建模为序贯智能体工作流，包含透明的五阶段流程：假设生成、图像锚定证据总结、证据条件化精炼、符号化验证与最终诊断。在克罗恩病（WCE 图像）与溃疡性结肠炎（B 超）两个独立患者队列上进行评估。

Result: ARISE 在两个队列上持续优于基线 VLM 的诊断性能，同时能揭示推理成功或失败的位置，为临床决策支持提供更可解释的依据。

Conclusion: ARISE 通过结构化、可解释的智能体框架提升了 IBD 影像诊断的表现与透明度，为临床决策支持和现实部署提供了更切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) requires frequent imaging-based assessment, yet interpretation of modalities such as wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) and intestinal ultrasound remains heavily dependent on specialist expertise. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate significant potential in multimodal medical image analysis, but their clinical adoption is hindered by their insufficient domain-specific reasoning, susceptibility to hallucination, scarcity of high quality training data in fine-grained diagnostics and limited interpretability. We introduce ARISE (Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounded Self-Evaluation), an autonomous planning framework that models few-shot medical image understanding as a sequential agentic workflow. ARISE structures agent execution into a transparent 5-stage workflow: hypothesis generation, image-grounded evidence summarization, evidence-conditioned refinement, symbolic verification, and final diagnosis. We apply ARISE to IBD assessment across two independent patient cohorts: wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) images for Crohn's disease and B-mode ultrasound data for ulcerative colitis. ARISE consistently improves diagnostic performance over baseline VLMs while exposing where reasoning succeeds or fails, providing a more interpretable basis for clinical decision support and realistic deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Super-Resolution in The Right Latent Space: A Frozen Vision-Foundation Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04781)
*Wanzhou Lei,Cuifeng Sheng,Yanjin He,Maohua Li,Hua Yuan,Per-Olof Persson,Hanlin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出在合适的潜空间中做超分，选用冻结DINOv3-L第23层融合特征的潜空间，训练415M解码器，一次前向即完成超分，在多个数据集上取得最佳保真-感知权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨自然清晰图像的嵌入在潜空间中构成流形，退化图像嵌入会偏离该流形，真实世界超分本质是将其映射回流形上保持语义与像素细节的忠实点。现有方法在重建导向的潜空间或像素空间中实现该映射，但低分辨退化图像嵌入离流形很远，映射困难且昂贵，且缺少语义信息，退化严重时易产生严重幻觉。因此选择合适的潜空间对超分至关重要。

Method: 作者发现冻结DINOv3-L的23个融合层的潜空间适合超分：退化图像嵌入靠近流形，且包含从像素记录到退化鲁棒语义的层级信息，能引导模型找到流形上的忠实点。在此基底上训练一个415M解码器，采用重建与对抗目标，将退化嵌入映射回并一次前向解码到像素空间，得到模型RAESR。

Result: 在RealSR、DRealSR、LSDIR和DIV2K-Val上，RAESR在最先进的对抗与扩散恢复方法中取得最佳保真-感知权衡；单张H20 GPU上每张512×512图像耗时37毫秒。

Conclusion: 合适潜空间的选择对超分任务至关重要。在相同配方下将基底替换为VAE潜空间，在所有指标上均表现更差，验证了DINOv3融合特征潜空间的优越性。

Abstract: In an image latent space, the embeddings of high-resolution, natural, and sharp images form a manifold. Degradation of high-resolution images pushes their embeddings off this manifold. Real-world super-resolution (SR) then becomes the task of mapping the degraded embedding back onto this manifold --- not anywhere on the manifold, but to the point that preserves what the input still carries, both its semantics and pixel details. Every published method implements this mapping in a reconstruction-oriented latent space or pixel space. We claim these spaces are the wrong substrates for SR. Low-resolution and degraded images are embedded far from the manifold, making the mapping difficult and expensive. The lack of semantic information in these substrates also makes it difficult to navigate to the faithful point on the manifold, causing severe hallucination when degradation is heavy. Thus, restoring in a suitable latent space is crucial to the SR task. We show that the latent space of 23 fused layers of a frozen DINOv3-L is one such space that makes the SR task easier. Degraded images are embedded near the manifold. Moreover, this substrate contains a hierarchy of information, from pixel record to degradation robust semantics, guiding the model to find the faithful point on the manifold. On this substrate, a 415M decoder is trained under reconstruction and adversarial objectives to map the degraded embeddings back and decode to pixel space in one pass. The resulting model, RAESR, attains the best fidelity--perception trade-off among state-of-the-art adversarial and diffusion-based restorers on RealSR, DRealSR, LSDIR and DIV2K-Val, at 37 ms per 512 by 512 image on a single H20 GPU. Swapping the substrate for a VAE latent under an identical recipe loses on every metric.

</details>


### [77] [Lollypop: Camera-to-Motion-Capture Calibration Verification with a Reference Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04785)
*Tianyi Liu,Kevin Harris,Mihika Dave,Kun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lollypop——一种用于相机-动捕标定独立验证的基准靶标，将ArUco视觉标记与动捕标记星座耦合，使视觉中心与追踪质心对应同一物理点，通过投影动捕点与检测到的基准中心之间的偏差来评估标定质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机到动捕（mocap）的标定在机器人、AR/VR等任务中被当作真值使用，但标定在部署后可能发生漂移，而标定残差与目视检查难以提供独立的验证手段，因此需要一种能够独立检验标定质量的方法。

Method: 设计Lollypop靶标：将ArUco基准标记与动捕标记星座耦合在一起，使二者的视觉中心与追踪质心代表同一物理点。给定候选标定后，将动捕点投影到图像中，测量其与检测到的基准中心之间的不一致程度，以此作为标定验证指标。

Result: 实验显示该方法的标称误差达到亚像素级，对受控的外参扰动具有敏感性，并在一个示例性的混合操作序列中表现出误差随时间的增长。

Conclusion: Lollypop提供了一种独立、灵敏的相机-动捕标定验证手段，可检测标定漂移，弥补了标定残差与目视检查在独立验证方面的不足。

Abstract: Camera-to-motion-capture (mocap) calibration is essential for using mocap as ground truth in robotics, AR/VR, and other computer vision tasks. However, the calibration can drift after deployment, while calibration residuals and visual inspection provide limited independent verification. We present Lollypop, a fiducial-mocap reference target for independent calibration verification. The target couples an ArUco fiducial with a mocap marker constellation so the visual center and tracked centroid represent the same physical point. Given a candidate calibration, verification projects the mocap point into the image and measures its disagreement with the detected fiducial center. Experiments show sub-pixel nominal error, sensitivity to controlled extrinsic perturbations, and increasing error during an illustrative mixed-handling sequence.

</details>


### [78] [Active-DiNTS: Active Differentiable Network Topology Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04787)
*Gean Trindade Pereira,Thierry Urruty,Muriel Visani,André C. P. L. F. de Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Active-DiNTS，将主动学习嵌入双层可微拓扑搜索，在单 GPU、少量标注下提升 3D 医学图像分割精度，搜索速度较 DiNTS 快 27 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索（NAS）是人工设计的替代方案，但应用于 3D 医学图像分割时受两大成本限制：标注预算大、需多 GPU 集群。因此需要一种能同时降低标注与算力需求的方法。

Method: 将基于池的主动学习（AL）嵌入双层可微拓扑搜索，实现架构发现与标签筛选的联合进行。每轮查询用三种不确定性信号（熵、方差、标准差）对未标注 MRI 体积排序，仅将排名靠前的样本送交标注。新标签驱动两个互锁阶段：外层循环更新网络权重，内层循环优化 U-Net 式主干的宏/微观拓扑。设置三种 AL 模式（仅权重、仅拓扑、联合）以揭示速度—精度权衡。

Result: 在 MSD Task01 BrainTumour 上，Active-DiNTS 在 Dice 上超过 DiNTS、C2FNAS 和 nnU-Net：Edema 约提升 10 个百分点，Non-Enhancing core 提升约 5 个百分点。仅用单 GPU 和部分标注量。发现的架构更稠密、FLOP 更高，但可训练参数与峰值内存仍有竞争力；最快的搜索变体在 0.25 GPU-days 内完成，比八卡 DiNTS 搜索快 27 倍以上。

Conclusion: 将可微 NAS 与主动数据获取相结合，是在现实约束下实现高精度 3D 分割的实用方案。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has proved to be a strong alternative to manual network design, but applying it to 3D medical image segmentation is limited by two well-known costs, large annotation budgets and multi-GPU clusters. Thus, this paper introduces Active-DiNTS (Active Differentiable Network Topology Search), an approach that embeds pool-based Active Learning (AL) into a bi-level differentiable topology search to perform architecture discovery and label curation jointly. At each query round, unlabeled MRI volumes are ranked by one of three uncertainty signals (Entropy, Variance, or Standard Deviation), and only the top-ranked volumes are sent to an oracle for annotation. The new labels feed two interlocked stages. Network weights are updated in an outer loop, while the macro/micro topology of a U-Net-style backbone is refined in an inner loop. Three AL regimes (weights-only, topology-only, joint) expose the speed-accuracy trade-off. Evaluations on the Medical Segmentation Decathlon (MSD) Task01 BrainTumour benchmark showed that Active-DiNTS surpasses DiNTS, C2FNAS, and nnU-Net in Dice, with gains of about 10 percentage points on Edema and 5 points on Non-Enhancing core, on a single GPU and using a fraction of the labeled volumes. The discovered architectures are denser and more FLOP-heavy than prior baselines, but remain competitive in trainable parameters and peak memory; the fastest search variant finishes in under 0.25 GPU-days, over 27x faster than the eight-GPU DiNTS search. Together, these results indicate that pairing differentiable NAS with active data acquisition is a practical recipe for accurate 3D segmentation under realistic constraints.

</details>


### [79] [MOXIE: Discovering Alternative Explanations for Biomedical Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04814)
*Abiha Tahsin Chowdhury,Rahul Dubey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOXIE是一个进化式多目标解释框架，用NSGA-II搜索能保持分类器置信度且尽量少用图像片段的片段子集，返回Pareto前沿式的多种解释，实验表明其在BloodMNIST和HAM10000上超体积均优于LIME，且分割方式显著影响评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIME等基于分割的解释方法对每个预测只返回单一解释，且依赖于一次固定分割。这掩盖了两个事实：同一预测可由多种不同片段集合支撑；分割方式本身决定了能发现哪些解释。因此需要能同时呈现多种替代解释并揭示分割影响的框架。

Method: 提出MOXIE（Multi-Objective eXplanation Imaging Engine），一个进化框架，搜索在保持分类器置信度的同时尽可能少保留图像的片段子集。它不返回单一解释，而是给出从紧凑到高保真的Pareto前沿替代解释。使用NSGA-II结合四种分割方法（SLIC、Felzenszwalb、Watershed、Voronoi），在BloodMNIST与HAM10000数据集上评估，预算与LIME相同。

Result: 在每张图像上，MOXIE的超体积均高于LIME。LIME的解释常常看起来令人信服，但仅展示其高亮片段时分类器置信度会崩塌。MOXIE的前沿揭示了保持模型置信度所需的图像比例，以及哪些上下文区域产生影响。分割方式强烈影响评估：片段大小不均的方法在按片段计数时显得最紧凑。

Conclusion: 替代解释能比单一解释更完整地呈现模型决策过程。分割方法本身是评估中的关键变量，须加以考虑；MOXIE通过Pareto前沿提供了更全面的解释视角。

Abstract: Segment-based explanation methods such as LIME return a single explanation for each prediction, computed from one fixed image segmentation. This hides two important facts: a prediction can be supported by many different sets of image segments, and the segmentation itself shapes which explanations can be found. We introduce MOXIE (Multi-Objective eXplanation Imaging Engine), an evolutionary framework that searches for segment subsets that preserve the classifier's confidence while keeping as little of the image as possible. Instead of one explanation, MOXIE returns a Pareto front of alternative explanations that range from compact to highly faithful. We evaluate MOXIE with NSGA-II and four segmentation methods (SLIC, Felzenszwalb, Watershed and Voronoi) on BloodMNIST and HAM10000 datasets, using the same evaluation budget as LIME. Results show that MOXIE achieves a higher hypervolume than LIME on every image. LIME's explanations often appear convincing, yet the classifier's confidence collapses when only the highlighted segments are shown. MOXIE's fronts reveal how much of the image is needed to preserve the model's confidence and which contextual regions influence it. We also find that segmentation strongly affects evaluation: methods with unequal segment sizes appear most compact when segments are counted. These results show that alternative explanations provide a more complete view of a model's decision than a single explanation.

</details>


### [80] [DriftSR: One-Step Real-World Image Super-Resolution via Distribution Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04819)
*Wei Zhu,Kai Zhang,Yu Zheng,Zhaopeng Yang,Lei Luo,Yong Guo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftSR 是一种一步式真实世界图像超分辨率框架，通过在预训练扩散模型的冻结中间表示空间中进行分布漂移，仅优化一步生成器，无需额外蒸馏分支或对抗判别器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步式 Real-ISR 推理高效，但要恢复逼真且感知丰富的细节通常依赖分数蒸馏或对抗学习，这引入了额外的可训练组件并使优化更加繁琐。因此需要一种既高效又无需额外组件的方案。

Method: 提出 DriftSR，利用预训练扩散先验通过分布漂移实现一步 Real-ISR。在冻结的扩散模型中间表示空间中进行漂移，不引入任务特定特征编码器；提出空间特征漂移（Spatial Feature Drifting），将空间特征而非整幅图像视为分布样本以获得更密集的分布对齐监督；并提出结构调制引导（Structure-Modulated Guidance），根据与低质量输入的局部结构一致性自适应地精炼漂移引导。

Result: 在三个真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，DriftSR 能实现高质量的超分辨率重建，并具备高效的一步推理能力。

Conclusion: DriftSR 仅优化一步生成器，无需辅助蒸馏分支或对抗判别器，即可在一步推理下实现高质量的 Real-ISR，验证了在冻结扩散表示空间中进行分布漂移的有效性。

Abstract: One-step real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) offers efficient inference, but recovering realistic and perceptually rich details often relies on score distillation or adversarial learning, introducing additional trainable components and making optimization more cumbersome. To this end, we propose DriftSR, a one-step Real-ISR framework that leverages pretrained diffusion priors through distribution drifting. Specifically, we perform drifting in the frozen intermediate representation space of a pretrained diffusion model, without introducing an additional task-specific feature encoder. Building on this space, we introduce Spatial Feature Drifting, which treats spatial features rather than entire images as distributional samples, enabling denser supervision for distribution alignment. To mitigate structural deviations, we further introduce Structure-Modulated Guidance, which adaptively refines drifting guidance according to local structural consistency with the LQ input. Consequently, DriftSR optimizes only the one-step generator, without auxiliary distillation branches or adversarial discriminators. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DriftSR delivers high-quality super-resolution reconstruction with efficient one-step inference.

</details>


### [81] [RSure-Agent: Reliable Use of Tool Observations for Remote Sensing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04836)
*Fuyuan Liu,Nayu Liu,Wenhao Yu,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Fanglong Yao,Liang Wan,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文分析遥感智能体中工具观测错误问题，提出RSure-Agent框架用于验证工具观测并抑制错误传播，在多个基准上显著降低错误传播率并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感智能体依赖感知、测量和栅格分析工具完成地球观测任务，但其对地物判断和定量结果（工具观测）存在较大不确定性，即使工具成功执行也可能出错。一旦智能体接受错误观测，错误会随推理传播导致任务失败。对1,229条执行轨迹的分析显示，各基准至少88.1%的任务依赖工具观测，其中至少22.7%含错误观测，且至少82.0%受影响任务的错误传播到最终答案，因此亟需验证工具观测并限制错误传播。

Method: 提出RSure-Agent框架。一是引入可验证观测协议，要求工具返回过程证据供智能体核验其观测；二是从离线任务反馈构建任务-工具可靠性先验，概括各工具配置在不同任务类型上的历史表现，为验证提供任务特定参考。基于过程证据与先验，RSure-Agent决定接受观测、请求补充证据或拒绝该观测。

Result: 在EarthBench、ThinkGeo、TerraLogic和CHOICE-420上评估。在三个智能体基准上，相比禁用验证与先验的基线配置，错误传播率降低21.3至25.9个百分点；在CHOICE-420上，11个主干模型平均整体准确率较直接回答提升5.71个百分点；在EarthBench上，相较Earth-Agent工具调用比率降低25.9%。

Conclusion: 工具观测错误在遥感智能体任务中普遍存在且高度传播，RSure-Agent通过过程证据验证与可靠性先验有效抑制错误传播，并提升任务准确率、降低工具调用开销，验证了观测验证机制的有效性。

Abstract: Remote sensing agents rely on perception, measurement, and raster analysis tools to solve Earth observation tasks. We refer to their judgments and quantitative results about ground objects as tool observations. However, these observations are subject to substantial uncertainty and may be incorrect even when the tools execute successfully. When agents accept incorrect observations, the errors can propagate through subsequent reasoning and cause task failure. We analyze 1,229 execution trajectories across three remote sensing agent benchmarks. On each benchmark, at least 88.1% of tasks depend on tool observations. Among these tasks, at least 22.7% contain incorrect observations despite successful tool execution. These errors propagate to the final answer in at least 82.0% of affected tasks on each benchmark. To address this problem, we propose RSure-Agent, a framework for verifying tool observations and limiting error propagation. We introduce a verifiable observation protocol that requires tools to return process evidence for the agent to verify their observations. We also construct a task-tool reliability prior from offline task feedback. The prior summarizes each tool configuration's past performance across task types and provides a task-specific reference for verification. Using process evidence and this prior, RSure-Agent decides whether to accept an observation, request additional evidence, or reject it. We evaluate RSure-Agent on EarthBench, ThinkGeo, TerraLogic, and CHOICE-420. Across the three agent benchmarks, RSure-Agent reduces the error propagation rate by 21.3 to 25.9 percentage points relative to the base configuration with verification and the prior disabled. On CHOICE-420, it improves overall accuracy over direct answering by 5.71 percentage points on average across 11 backbone models. On EarthBench, it reduces the tool-call ratio by 25.9% relative to Earth-Agent.

</details>


### [82] [One Tile, Multiple Instances: Rethinking MIL for Sparse Diagnostic Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04853)
*Runsheng Liu,Cheng Jin,Hao Jiang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DI-MIL框架，通过分解实例将编码上下文与实例粒度解耦，在弱监督全切片图像分类中显著提升性能且无需额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弱监督全切片图像分类中，特征提取器将每个图像块压缩为单一全局嵌入，导致切片级聚合器仅能在粗粒度块尺度上工作，掩盖了块内细粒度证据，使注意力机制无法捕捉稀疏的诊断信号。

Method: DI-MIL通过聚合同一图像块内来自冻结基础模型的稠密空间令牌，将单个块转换为多个可独立加权的实例嵌入。作为免训练的后编码模块，它可无缝集成到现有流程中，无需重新编码或修改下游架构。

Result: 在细胞病理学上评估，跨4个数据集、3个冻结基础模型和2个注意力聚合器，DI-MIL在72项指标比较中改善67项，最大平均增益达3.64点；与7个代表性MIL基线相比，配对ACMIL在36项比较中33项取得最高平均性能。消融显示直接缩小分块会使块数膨胀高达43.3倍且性能非单调，而DI-MIL零额外图像提取开销。

Conclusion: 研究表明实例构建是MIL中一个正交的设计维度，DI-MIL是稀疏诊断证据下具有成本效益的解决方案。

Abstract: In weakly supervised Whole Slide Image (WSI) classification, feature extractors typically compress each image tile into a single global embedding. Consequently, slide-level aggregators are restricted to this coarse tile scale, concealing fine-grained sub-tile evidence from the attention mechanism. We introduce DI-MIL, a framework that decouples encoding context from instance granularity through decomposed instances. By clustering dense spatial tokens from a frozen foundation model within each tile, DI-MIL converts a single tile into multiple independently weighted instance embeddings. As a training-free post-encoding module, DI-MIL integrates seamlessly into existing pipelines without requiring re-encoding or downstream architectural modifications. We evaluate DI-MIL on cytopathology, a challenging testbed where sparse diagnostic signals are easily diluted within standard tiles. Across four datasets, three frozen foundation models, and two attention-based aggregators, DI-MIL demonstrates consistent efficacy, improving 67 of 72 metric-level comparisons, with the largest mean gains reaching 3.64 points under cytopathology-specific backbones. In a broader comparison against seven representative MIL baselines, DI-MIL paired with ACMIL achieves highest mean performance in 33 of 36 backbone-dataset-metric comparisons. Ablations show that direct smaller tiling inflates the extracted tile count by up to 43.3$\times$ with non-monotonic performance, whereas DI-MIL incurs zero additional image-extraction overhead while achieving the strongest overall results. These results establish instance construction as an orthogonal design dimension in MIL, supporting DI-MIL as a cost-efficient solution under sparse diagnostic evidence.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Training Numerical Intelligence via Auto-Diagnosis and Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03872)
*Peter Chen,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ADSD框架，将数值诊断与可复用求解器自我改进相连接，实现从"试错编辑"到"诊断-发现-实现"的结构化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体虽能生成科学代码，但生成代码不等于改进算法。对数值求解器而言，执行反馈能暴露性能不佳，却很少揭示根本原因及改进方法。

Method: ADSD采用"诊断优先"范式：先解释求解器表现不佳的原因，再用该诊断指导发现合适的数值方法，并将所得知识封装为可复用的求解器技能。

Result: 在潮流方程、交流最优潮流控制、刚性常微分方程、异质扩散偏微分方程四个数值领域均一致提升求解器精度、鲁棒性与效率。在GOC-500潮流任务上，平均求解误差降低近71倍，并进一步迁移到未见过的电网拓扑与运行工况。

Conclusion: ADSD将求解器改进从试错式编辑转变为结构化过程，改进效果可迁移，表明诊断驱动的方法对数值求解器自我提升具有有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: AI agents are becoming increasingly capable of generating scientific code, but generating code is not the same as improving the algorithms behind it. For numerical solvers, execution feedback can expose poor performance, but rarely reveals its underlying cause and how to address it. We introduce Auto-Diagnosis and Skill Discovery (ADSD), a framework that links numerical diagnosis to reusable solver self-improvement. ADSD follows a diagnosis-first paradigm that first explains why a solver performs poorly, then uses this diagnosis to guide the discovery of appropriate numerical methods. The resulting knowledge is packaged into reusable solver skills, turning solver improvement from trial-and-error editing into a structured process of diagnosis, discovery, and implementation. Across four challenging numerical domains--power flow equation, AC optimal power flow control, stiff ordinary differential equations, and heterogeneous diffusion PDEs--ADSD consistently improves solver accuracy, robustness, and efficiency. On GOC-500 power flow, for example, ADSD reduces mean solver error by nearly $71\times$, with improvements further transferring to unseen grid topologies and operating regimes.

</details>


### [84] [REACT: Physically and Chemically Consistent Reconstruction of Marine Active Tracers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03888)
*Wenbin Dai,Hao Zheng,Shiyu Liang,Chaofan Sun,Xueying Zhang,Hanbo Huang,Xuan Gong,Yiran Zhang,Enhui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REACT——一种“碳优先”的海表pH重建框架，通过解耦输运、活性校正与化学解码三步，在保持pH为预测目标的同时约束底层碳状态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同化与反演模型物理基础扎实但代价高昂；现有黑箱和物理引导AI模型虽高效，却主要针对被动示踪物设计，即重建变量本身就是被输运的量。而pH是主动碳酸盐示踪物，其预测目标（pH）与守恒碳库存（DIC）不一致，易出现pH误差低但违反碳酸盐闭合和无源碳守恒的问题。

Method: REACT将重建解耦为三部分：用保守的平流-扩散求解器输运潜在碳酸盐状态；用源模块捕捉非保守的碳循环变化；将校正后的状态解码为pH，并通过碳酸盐平衡约束输出。该设计在保持pH为目标的同时对底层碳状态施加一致性。

Result: 在模拟数据上，REACT相较最优基线将pH NRMSE降低14.7%，化学一致性误差降低24.0%。跨时间尺度评估显示其对从粗到细时间尺度上的误差累积具有鲁棒性，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: REACT通过“碳优先”的解耦建模，兼顾了重建精度与碳化学一致性，为主动碳酸盐示踪物的大尺度海表pH重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing global sea surface pH from sparse observations is critical for monitoring ocean acidification and understanding marine carbon cycling. Traditional assimilation and inverse models are physically grounded but costly for large-scale reconstruction. Recent black-box and physics-guided AI models improve efficiency, but are mainly designed for passive tracers, where the reconstructed variable is also the transported inventory. In contrast, pH is an active carbonate tracer: it is the prediction target, while dissolved inorganic carbon (DIC) is the conserved carbon inventory. This mismatch can produce low pH error while violating carbonate closure and source-free carbon conservation. To address this, we introduce \textbf{REACT}, a carbon-first reconstruction framework that decouples transport, active correction, and chemical decoding. REACT transports a latent carbonate state with a conservative advection--diffusion solver, captures non-conservative carbon-cycle variations with a source module, decodes the corrected state into pH, and constrains the output through carbonate equilibrium. This design keeps pH as the target while enforcing consistency on the underlying carbon state. On simulation data, REACT reduces pH NRMSE by (14.7%) and chemical consistency error by (24.0%) over the best baseline. Cross-temporal-scale evaluations show robustness against error accumulation from coarse to fine temporal scales, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [85] [Proxy Confidence: Auditing Black-Box LLM Agents with a Surrogate's Log-Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03894)
*Yikai Zhao,Saurabh Pandey,Pradeep Kumar Misra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需训练、低成本的代理模型（surrogate）方法，通过并行运行一个开放权重的模型来读取LLM代理工具调用的日志概率，从而检测其隐蔽错误，并在多个基准上显著优于代理自述置信度和自一致性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的LLM代理会产生工具调用、查询和代码，这些操作可能在不知不觉中出错，等到错误暴露时操作已经执行。前沿聊天API隐藏了模型的token概率，代理自述的置信度在关键错误上几乎与随机猜测无异；而重采样也无济于事，因为前沿模型高度重复，多次采样会复现相同的调用。因此亟需一种能恢复缺失可靠性信号的方法。

Method: 利用一个低成本的开放权重代理模型并行运行，读取与代理相同的上下文、模式和拟议动作，通过其自身日志概率进行多类互补判读：教师强制（teacher forcing）与请求PMI衡量每个参数值的似然，判别式裁决（discriminative verdict）整体判断调用，工具选择竞争（tool-choice competition）将函数与其同类进行比较。原则是：生成式似然定位错误的参数值，而裁决捕获整体错误的调用；当错误类型未知时，集成是低遗憾的默认选择。该方法无需训练、无需访问代理内部，仅需一次预填充（prefill）。

Result: 在困难编程任务上达到AUROC 0.825，而代理自述置信度接近随机（0.598）；生成式判读在另外三个代理上比其高出+0.07到+0.28。相比自一致性，在近乎确定性的代理上提升+0.14到+0.19，成本仅为1/K。该信号驱动两种部署模式：实时门控将最不可信的调用升级审查（在50%覆盖率下接受的行动准确率提升+0.05到+0.30）；置信反馈将工具结果连同分数返回，使代理调整下一步（在实时执行基准上任务成功率提升+0.119和+0.137，p ≤ 1e-4），并在步骤错误隐蔽的场景下击败随机值对照（+0.078，p = 0.003）。

Conclusion: 通过低成本开放权重代理模型的日志概率，可在无需训练和访问代理内部的情况下，有效检测LLM代理工具调用的隐蔽错误，并提供实时门控与置信反馈两种实用部署模式，显著提升行动准确率与任务成功率。

Abstract: A deployed LLM agent emits tool calls, queries, and code that can be silently wrong -- by the time the error surfaces, the action has run. Frontier chat APIs hide the model's token probabilities; the agent's stated confidence barely beats chance on the mistakes that matter; and resampling does not help, since frontier models are highly repetitive, reproducing the same call across samples.
  We recover the missing signal from a low-cost open-weight surrogate run in parallel. It reads the same context, schema, and proposed action as the agent, then scores the call from its own log-probabilities through a family of complementary readouts: teacher forcing and request-PMI weigh the likelihood of each argument value, a discriminative verdict judges the call as a whole, and tool-choice competition tests the function against its siblings. One principle says which to trust: a generative likelihood localizes wrong argument values, while the verdict catches holistically wrong calls. When the error type is unknown, an ensemble is the low-regret default. The readout is training-free, needs no access to the agent's internals, and costs one prefill pass alongside the tool call.
  On difficult coding tasks it reaches AUROC 0.825 where the actor's stated confidence is near chance (0.598), and the generative readouts beat it by +0.07 to +0.28 across three further actors. Against self-consistency it gains +0.14 to +0.19 on near-deterministic actors, at 1/K the cost. The signal drives two deployment modes: a real-time gate escalating the least-trustworthy calls for review (+0.05 to +0.30 accepted-action accuracy at 50% coverage), and confidence feedback, returning the tool result with the score so the agent adapts its next step -- lifting task success on live-execution benchmarks (+0.119 and +0.137, p <= 1e-4) and beating a random-value control where step errors are silent (+0.078, p = 0.003).

</details>


### [86] [SGAnalog: An End-to-End Circuit Benchmark from Open-Source Silicon Tapeouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03934)
*Yueting Li,Weihang Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于开源Tiny Tapeout流片电路的新基准，包含273个拓扑不同的顶层设计，评测原理图到网表转写与器件尺寸设计两项任务，揭示模型在跨任务排名上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟集成电路设计基准难以回答两个问题：模型是否学到了可迁移的电路技能而非仅回忆熟悉样例；其输出能否在明确的工艺与测试条件下工作。为此需要一个来源可追溯、可复现、带结构与仿真上下文的基准。

Method: 从与Tiny Tapeout制造班次相关的开源人工设计电路构建基准，共273个拓扑不同的顶层设计。每个源文件按其班次提交记录的版本获取，并在固定容器化环境中处理。流水线从同一源文件导出原理图图像与SPICE网表，为转写提供精确结构参照；提交日期支持按模型训练截止点分析；作者测试平台提供尺寸设计的仿真上下文。评测原理图到网表转写和器件尺寸设计两项任务。

Result: 在固定66个转写任务上，七个模型中最强者达到56.1%精确图同构，且七个中有六个从小规模到中等规模任务骤降。对某前沿模型，去除作者选定标签会降低精确匹配但保持总体结构F1，说明标签有助于连通性追踪。在17个尺寸设计任务上，领先模型对全部17个提案收敛，取得91.2/100分；而两个最新Claude模型在相同提示下分别拒答4个和11个（这些提示在转写时它们无障碍处理）；无尺寸的提案得零分。两项任务产生不同模型排名，暴露出不同的视觉与设计能力，并在某一模型家族中表现为策略而非能力限制。

Conclusion: 该基准通过同源原理图/网表参照、训练截止分析与作者测试平台，实现了可追溯、可复现的模拟电路评测。转写与尺寸设计两项任务排名不一致，说明二者考查不同的视觉与设计能力；同时观察到某模型家族的拒答属于策略限制而非能力不足。

Abstract: Existing analog integrated circuit design benchmarks make two questions hard to answer: whether a model has learned transferable circuit skills rather than recalled familiar examples, and whether its output works under defined process and test conditions. We introduce a benchmark built from human-designed, open-source circuits associated with Tiny Tapeout manufacturing shuttles. The collection contains 273 topologically distinct top-level designs. Every source is retrieved at the revision recorded for its shuttle submission and processed in a fixed containerized environment. The pipeline exports each eligible schematic image and its SPICE netlist from the same source file, giving transcription an exact structural reference. Commit dates support model-specific training-cutoff analysis, while author testbenches provide the simulation context for sizing. The benchmark evaluates schematic-to-netlist transcription and device sizing. Across seven models on a fixed set of 66 transcription tasks, the strongest model reaches 56.1% exact graph isomorphism, and six of seven models drop sharply from the small to the medium tier. For one frontier model, removing author-chosen labels reduces exact matches while preserving aggregate structural F1, suggesting that labels can aid connectivity tracing. On the 17 sizing tasks, the leading model converges on all 17 proposals and reaches 91.2 out of 100 against the human reference, while the two newest Claude models refuse 4 and 11 of the same prompts they transcribe without objection; a proposal without sizes scores zero. The two tasks produce different model rankings, exposing distinct visual and design capabilities and, in one family, a policy rather than capability limit.

</details>


### [87] [MLLMs Fail to Refuse when Using Tools Agentically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03938)
*Rikiya Takehi,Ryo Hachiuma,Shaona Ghosh,Dan Zhao,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Yusuke Hirota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了智能体式多模态大模型在工具使用场景下拒绝有害请求能力显著下降的安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式多模态大模型通过调用缩放、标注等工具推动了视觉推理的前沿，但作者发现工具使用范式存在关键的安全缺陷：使用工具时模型更难以拒绝有害请求。

Method: 在三个常用安全基准上，对多个主流的开源和闭源权重多模态大模型进行对比测试，比较其在使用工具与非工具设置下的安全性，并分析超过10万条回复及扩展实验。

Result: 所有测试模型在工具使用设置下的安全性均显著低于非工具设置，相对拒绝失败率最高上升68.7%。

Conclusion: 智能体式工具使用会削弱多模态大模型的安全拒绝能力，作者基于大规模回复分析提出了两种可能导致该安全退化的原因。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently pushed the frontier of visual reasoning by calling tools such as zooming and tagging. Despite the recent strong success of agentic MLLMs, this work uncovers a critical safety failure in the tool-use paradigm: agentic tool-using MLLMs become less capable of refusing harmful requests. Our experiments confirm that, across three popular safety benchmarks, all the top open- and closed-weight MLLMs we test exhibit significantly lower safety in tool-using settings than in non-tool settings, with a relative refusal failure rate increase of up to 68.7%. Based on analysis of 100,000+ responses, including extended experiments, we also propose two possible reasons for this safety degradation.

</details>


### [88] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Model Decision-Making for Autonomous Driving Guided by Chinese Philosophical Wisdom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03948)
*Xiaojun Bi,Xiaoyuan Ma,Yiwen Sun,Tianren Huang,Chaoran Liu,Bokai Huang,Hao Yang,Baichuan Mo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CPW-Drive框架，将中国哲学价值原则通过闭环RAG引入自动驾驶决策，并设计物理感知空间相似度检索(PSSR)，在Highway-env多车道任务上成功率最高达93.0%，显著优于最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶决策方法（数值优化、序列预测、大语言模型）主要关注安全与效率，对哲学与伦理价值层面的考量有限，缺乏对复杂交通交互中社会规范的价值引导。

Method: CPW-Drive是一个闭环检索增强生成(RAG)框架：以中国儒家思想为知识源，利用LLM从经典文本抽取关键词，经人工筛选与验证整合为与驾驶相关的价值原则，并通过场景化案例构建可检索、可复用的价值引导；同时提出物理感知空间相似度检索(PSSR)，通过比较车辆布局与速度外推状态来检索物理相关的历史案例。

Result: 在Highway-env多车道高速公路驾驶任务上，CPW-Drive在三种交通配置下成功率分别为93.0%、86.0%、72.0%，比最强基线高出8.0、22.5、25.0个百分点；在所有配置下取得最高无碰撞步数，并保持较低的变道频率。

Conclusion: 结构化的价值引导能够提升模拟闭环场景下的安全性与稳定性，但同时会带来效率与延迟方面的权衡。

Abstract: Autonomous driving decision systems must balance safety, efficiency, and social norms in complex traffic interactions. Philosophical and ethical considerations have received limited attention in existing autonomous driving decision-making approaches based on numerical optimization, sequence prediction, and large language models (LLMs). We propose Chinese Philosophical Wisdom-Guided Driving (CPW-Drive), a closed-loop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that incorporates value guidance derived from Chinese philosophy into autonomous driving decision-making. Using Chinese Confucian thought as its knowledge source, CPW-Drive consolidates LLM-extracted keywords from relevant classical texts into driving-relevant value principles through manual screening and validation. It then contextualizes these principles through scenario-specific cases to form retrievable and reusable value guidance. We further propose Physics-aware Spatial Similarity Retrieval (PSSR), which compares vehicle layouts and velocity-extrapolated states to retrieve physically relevant historical cases. On Highway-env's multilane highway-driving task, CPW-Drive achieves success rates of 93.0%, 86.0%, and 72.0% across three traffic configurations. These results outperform the strongest baseline by 8.0, 22.5, and 25.0 percentage points, respectively. Across all configurations, CPW-Drive achieves the highest collision-free step count and maintains a low lane-change frequency. The results suggest that structured value guidance can improve simulated closed-loop safety and stability while introducing efficiency and latency trade-offs.

</details>


### [89] [LatentQuant: Preserving the Policy-Facing Latent Contract under NVFP4 VAE Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03959)
*Ziye Deng,Lufang Chen,Shuyu Feng,Zhenwei Duan,Zicong Ye,Yu Sun,Xiaofan Li,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LatentQuant，一种两阶段NVFP4量化感知训练框架，通过先对齐量化编码器与高精度编码器、再冻结编码器适配解码器，解决world action models中VAE量化导致策略性能崩溃的问题，在保持重建质量的同时几乎无损地维持控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world action models（WAMs）复用预训练视频VAE，其编码器潜变量直接作为下游动作策略的输入条件。量化不仅要保持重建保真度，还必须维持冻结策略所期望的潜变量契约。直接NVFP4存在W4A4量化误差未补偿的问题，而联合QAT虽能恢复重建质量，却会破坏策略性能（LIBERO成功率从95.5%骤降至10.5%），因此需要一种兼顾重建与控制性能的量化方案。

Method: 作者通过受控的decoder-only实验揭示了机制：激活的量化-反量化操作会改变重建信号与解码器Jacobian，从而改变回传给编码器的梯度，引发持续的潜变量漂移。基于此机理提出LatentQuant——两阶段NVFP4 QAT框架：第一阶段使量化编码器与其高精度对应物对齐，第二阶段冻结编码器，仅适配解码器。

Result: 在Wan2.1和Wan2.2上，LatentQuant保持了接近基线的控制性能和高重建质量：LIBERO成功率达95.75%，RoboTwin达68.8%。在NVIDIA B300 GPU上，NVFP4执行相对BF16 cuDNN实现1.17x-1.26x的端到端VAE加速。

Conclusion: LatentQuant通过显式对齐编码器潜变量契约并冻结编码器，有效解决了量化导致策略潜变量漂移的问题，在实现NVFP4量化加速的同时几乎无损地维持下游动作策略性能，为WAMs的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent world action models (WAMs) reuse pretrained video VAEs whose encoder latents directly condition downstream action policies. Quantization must therefore preserve not only reconstruction fidelity but also the policy-facing latent contract expected by the frozen policy. Direct NVFP4 leaves W4A4 quantization error uncompensated, whereas joint quantization-aware training (QAT) can recover reconstruction by moving this representation. On Wan2.1, joint QAT nearly matches FP32 VBench-7 (0.7403 versus 0.7409), yet LIBERO success collapses from 95.5% to 10.5%. Controlled decoder-only experiments show that activation quantize-dequantize operations alter the reconstruction signal and decoder Jacobian, redirecting the gradient returned to the encoder and inducing persistent latent drift. Based on this mechanism, we introduce LatentQuant, a two-stage NVFP4 QAT framework that first aligns the quantized encoder with its high-precision counterpart, then freezes it while adapting the decoder. Across Wan2.1 and Wan2.2, LatentQuant preserves near-baseline control and high reconstruction quality, achieving 95.75% success on LIBERO and 68.8% on RoboTwin. On NVIDIA B300 GPUs, NVFP4 execution achieves 1.17x-1.26x end-to-end VAE speedups over BF16 cuDNN.

</details>


### [90] [ROAR: Unifying Runs across Heterogeneous AI-Driven Research Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03966)
*Leo Y. Lin,Vishakha Ramani,Z. Berkay Celik,Paul Castro,Marquita Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ROAR，一套用于统一并分析异构AI驱动研究系统（ADRS）输出数据的基础设施，通过关系模式与解析层解决格式异构与分析目标差异问题，并基于900+次运行的语料展示汇聚数据的分析价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADRS每次运行都是对庞大解空间的昂贵搜索，可靠评估需大量运行，因此运行数据既昂贵又有保留价值。但当前数据高度碎片化：团队各自孤立运作、不同框架输出格式不一，缺乏共享基础设施来跨问题与系统聚合或比较运行结果，导致跨系统的深层规律难以被发现。

Method: ROAR通过关系模式（relational schema）和解析层（parsing layer）来规范化异构ADRS输出，同时保留数据血缘与时间结构，并支持在不修改模式的前提下接入新系统。该设计应对两大挑战：一是调和异构输出，二是支持在目标与评分函数不同的运行间进行分析。作者据此构建了包含多个ADRS、900余次运行的语料库。

Result: 基于汇聚语料发现若干难以从单一系统观察到的特性：与既有工作一致，相同配置的运行可能收敛到不同分数；许多运行在早期即获得大部分收益；将先验解纳入搜索过程的策略有效性因问题而异。此外，作者利用ROAR来配置ADRS运行，证明该语料不仅可用于分析，还具有可操作性。

Conclusion: 汇聚的ADRS数据能揭示搜索行为中依赖问题的结构，这些洞见难以从任何单一系统、团队或基准中获得。只要运行数据仍处于孤立状态，这类跨切面洞见便难以获取；ROAR是首个专为统一此类数据而设计的基础设施。

Abstract: Each run of an AI-driven research system (ADRS) is an expensive search over a vast solution space, and dependable evaluation requires many runs, making run data both costly to produce and valuable to retain for large-scale analysis. Yet this data remains fragmented: teams operate in isolation, ADRS frameworks emit results in different formats, and no shared infrastructure exists to aggregate or compare runs across problems and systems. We present ROAR, a solution for systematically unifying and analyzing heterogeneous ADRS outputs. ROAR addresses two challenges: reconciling heterogeneous ADRS outputs and enabling analytics across runs with different objectives and scoring functions. We achieve this through a relational schema and parsing layer that normalize heterogeneous ADRS outputs while preserving data lineage and temporal structure, and accommodating new systems without requiring schema modifications. From building a corpus of more than 900 runs from multiple ADRS, we show how pooled data can reveal properties of problem landscapes that are difficult to observe. Consistent with prior work, runs with identical configurations may converge to different scores. We find that many runs realize most gains early, and that the effectiveness of different strategies for incorporating prior solutions into the search process varies across problems. We further show that the pooled corpus is actionable and not merely analytical by using ROAR to configure ADRS runs. Together, these results illustrate how pooled ADRS data can expose problem-dependent structure in search behavior that is difficult to detect from any single system, team, or benchmark. Such cross-cutting insights are difficult to obtain while runs remain siloed; ROAR is the first infrastructure designed to unify them.

</details>


### [91] [Teaching Agents to Code Reliably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03984)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Myeongsoo Kim,Kangrui Ruan,Shweta Garg,Varun Kumar,Murali Krishna Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究自主编码智能体解决仓库级问题的行为，提出三种关键行为（定位多样性、编辑多样性、验证可靠性）可通过训练而非仅靠扩展规模获得，并通过SFT与RL训练将行为内化到策略中，显著提升解题率与验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时算力只有在产生有用修复并提供可靠选择证据时才有收益。当前三种行为存在缺陷：定位多样性狭窄（反复回到同一位置，增加采样无收益）；编辑多样性未被利用（不同方法可解决互补问题）；验证误导（智能体自写的测试会接受大量错误补丁）。作者认为这些行为是可教的，可内化进策略而非依赖外部脚手架。

Method: 用执行反馈指导搜索，并将每个补丁与其自身的回退树（reverted tree）对比打分。随后通过训练将行为移入策略：先用加权监督微调（weighted SFT），再用强化学习目标，针对金标修复与错误变体训练验证器，并对能检测错误变体的断言给予信用奖励。

Result: 仅靠执行反馈与回退树打分的方法在SWE-bench Verified上解决52.8%的问题，仅用八样本基线的48.1%智能体步数。在270个留出问题上，加权SFT将pass@1从31.9%提至35.2%、pass@8从46.7%提至51.1%；RL进一步将pass@1提至43.0%、pass@8提至60.7%，验证器精度从26.8%升至41.7%，误接受减半以上。在三个分布外测试集中，两个解题率提升、三个验证精度全部提升，且在7B、14B、30B模型上相对公开编码基线均成立。

Conclusion: 定位、编辑与验证三类行为是可训练的技能而非规模的自然产物，将其内化进策略能在更少步数下取得更高解题率与更可靠的验证，且增益可迁移到分布外任务与不同模型规模。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents solve repository issues by reading code, running commands, editing files, and submitting patches. Extra inference-time compute yields gains only when it produces a useful repair and supplies reliable evidence for choosing one. Three behaviors decide both, and we argue they are teachable rather than byproducts of scale, so a policy can carry them instead of a scaffold. Location diversity remains narrow, since attempts return to the same site and extra samples add no coverage. Edit diversity is left unexploited, since methodologies that differ resolve complementary issues no single run reaches. Verification misleads, since a test the agent writes for its own patch accepts many incorrect ones. Directing search by execution feedback and scoring each patch against its own reverted tree resolves 52.8% of SWE-bench Verified using 48.1% of the agent-steps an eight-sample baseline spends. Training moves these behaviors into the policy. On the 270 issues held out from SFT and RL training, weighted supervised fine-tuning raises pass@1 from 31.9% to 35.2% and pass@8 from 46.7% to 51.1%. A reinforcement objective then trains the verifier against gold-labeled repairs and incorrect variants, crediting the assertions that detect them. It raises pass@1 to 43.0% and pass@8 to 60.7%, lifts verifier precision from 26.8% to 41.7%, and more than halves false acceptance. Resolution improves on two of three out-of-distribution suites and verifier precision on all three, and the gains hold at 7B, 14B, and 30B against published coder baselines.

</details>


### [92] [Behavioral History Outperforms Descriptions of the Person for LLM Synthetic Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03998)
*Khashayar Pourtaheri,Ahmad Zareei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了五种信息条件下LLM合成人格预测个体后续选择的能力，发现受访者过去的行为历史比人口统计、人格特质等描述信息更能提升个体层面的预测有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用作代表调查受访者的合成人格，但其作为特定受访者替代品的有效性取决于能否复现个体决策。研究旨在探究哪些信息有助于合成受访者预测每个个体的后续选择。

Method: 使用845名美国成年人的两波面板数据，测量涵盖风险、时间偏好、过度自信和推理的14种行为偏差。设计五种逐步增加信息量的条件：无个人信息、人口统计、人格特质、认知分数、受访者早期选择作为行为历史。以人类重测作为基准，在行为历史条件下，所有用于评估目标偏差的条目均被保留（扣留）。

Result: 总体层面，各条件的平均偏差数（7.1-8.1）接近人类（7.1）。但聚合相似性掩盖了方差差异：描述型人格仅恢复53-67%的人类个体间变异，加入行为历史后恢复至接近人类水平。个体层面，描述型人格仅达到人类重测信息量的7-12%，加入行为历史后升至28%。包含行为历史的条件在全部17个人口统计组中信息量估计最高，而描述型条件对某些组几乎没有信息。合成回应还表现出比人类更强的教育-收入相关差异。

Conclusion: 对LLM合成人格而言，受访者过去的答案对个体层面预测的贡献大于对其身份的描述。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as synthetic personas representing survey respondents. Their validity as substitutes for particular respondents depends on whether they reproduce individuals' decisions. We examine what information helps synthetic respondents predict each individual's later choices, using five conditions that add progressively richer information: no personal information, demographics, personality traits, cognitive scores, and finally the respondent's earlier survey choices as behavioral history. We use a two-wave panel of 845 US adults who completed measures of 14 behavioral biases (spanning risk, time preferences, overconfidence, and reasoning), so each respondent's earlier answers provide a human test-retest benchmark; in the behavioral-history condition, all items that score the target bias are withheld. At the population level, the average number of biases per respondent in every condition is close to the human average (7.1-8.1 biases, against 7.1 for humans). This aggregate similarity masks differences in variance: persona descriptions recover only 53-67% of human between-person variation, whereas adding behavioral history restores it to approximately the human level. At the individual level, description-based personas achieve only 7-12% of the informedness observed in human test-retest responses, while adding behavioral history raises this to 28%. The condition including behavioral history has the highest estimated informedness in all 17 demographic groups, whereas description-based conditions provide little or no information for some groups. Synthetic responses also exhibit stronger education- and income-related differences than human responses. For LLM synthetic personas, a respondent's past answers add more to individual-level prediction than a description of who they are.

</details>


### [93] [SkillScriptBench: Benchmarking Self-Evolution of Executable Agent Skill Packages Beyond Markdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04008)
*Yuxuan Liu,Haoran Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Hongyu Luo,Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Kawai Chung,Junle Chen,Changxuan Fan,Qing Zong,Lingyun Xie,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SkillScriptBench 基准（350个任务），将文档修复、脚本修复与保持正确行为分开评估，并进一步提出 AST-Guided Skill Revision 方法，利用抽象语法树与调用关系定位代码、约束脚本编辑并同步更新文档，在多个模型上显著提升修复成功率与多次运行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可执行 Agent Skills 将自然语言指令与脚本打包复用，修订时既要修正错误又不能破坏正确行为。现有基准无法系统区分文档修复、脚本修复以及对正确行为的保持，导致评估缺乏针对性。

Method: 构建 SkillScriptBench 基准：从 35,000+ GitHub Skill 根目录中筛选 100 个包，构造 150 个修复任务（含注入脚本故障的包、维护请求与可执行行为检查）；另有受控轨道 200 个任务（50 包），在干净、文档故障、脚本故障、双重故障四种状态下用同一维护请求评估。并提出 AST-Guided Skill Revision：基于抽象语法树和调用关系将维护需求映射到相关代码位置，限定脚本编辑范围，并同步更新文档。

Result: 在四个 LLM 上，同时编辑文档与脚本的方法能修复脚本故障，但在文档修复或行为保持上并未稳定优于仅改 Markdown 的方法。AST-Guided Skill Revision 平均提升修复成功率：Raw Package 提高 21.9%，CoEvoSkills 提高 27.7%；三次运行全部解决的任务比例分别提升 20.8% 和 31.5%。

Conclusion: 将技能修订按文档修复、脚本修复与保持行为拆分评估十分必要；AST 引导的修订可更精准地定位与约束编辑，显著提升修复成功率与重复运行的一致性。

Abstract: Executable Agent Skills combine natural-language instructions and scripts into reusable packages for LLM agents, and revising them requires fixing errors without breaking correct behavior. Existing benchmarks do not systematically distinguish documentation repair, script repair, and preservation when evaluating skill self-evolution. We introduce SkillScriptBench, a 350-task benchmark designed to evaluate these capabilities separately. From a survey of over 35,000 GitHub-hosted Skill roots, we select 100 packages and construct 150 repair tasks. Each task pairs a package containing injected script faults with a maintenance request and executable checks of the required behavior. A complementary controlled track contains 200 tasks from 50 packages, each evaluated under the same maintenance request in four states: clean, documentation faults, script faults, and faults in both. Across four LLMs, methods that edit both documentation and scripts can repair script faults but do not consistently outperform Markdown-only revision on documentation repair or preservation. We therefore introduce AST-Guided Skill Revision, which uses abstract syntax trees and calling relationships to link maintenance requirements to relevant code locations. It restricts script edits to these locations and updates the documentation to match the revised scripts. Averaged across models, this revision stage yields absolute gains in repair success of 21.9% for Raw Package and 27.7% for CoEvoSkills on faulty packages. Absolute gains in the proportion of tasks solved in all three runs reach 20.8% and 31.5%, respectively, indicating more consistent repair success across repeated runs.

</details>


### [94] [Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04011)
*Hangzhan jin,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization (ExPPO)，一种轻量级优势塑形规则，通过基于提示相对的、长度归一化的响应惊奇度与提示通过率重分配信用，在保持验证器极性与总绝对优势质量的同时提升推理探索覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励强化学习能提升推理能力，但学习信号的分配决定了重复采样下哪些解仍可被探索到。组相对目标给同等奖励的响应分配相同优势，使聚合信用与采样模式频率成正比，可能导致对低频正确模式的探索不足。

Method: ExPPO 采用提示相对、长度归一化的响应惊奇度以及提示通过率来重分配优势信用，结合有界塑形与共享归一化，保持验证器极性，并近似维持每个提示组的总绝对序列优势质量。分析上刻画响应级信用分配与采样模式更新，推导熵与正确模式发现增益的局部条件。

Result: 实验显示域内与域外推理覆盖提升，聚合响应准确率更高，在大采样预算下覆盖较强。受控多答案评估进一步表明正确模式产量增加，且经验证正确的响应多样性提升。

Conclusion: ExPPO 通过优势塑形改善探索保持与正确模式发现，在多个指标上优于基线，并在大采样预算下保持强覆盖能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning, while the allocation of learning signal shapes which solutions remain accessible under repeated sampling. Group-relative objectives assign equal advantages to equally rewarded responses, making aggregate credit proportional to sampled mode frequency. We introduce Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization (ExPPO), a lightweight advantage-shaping rule that redistributes credit using prompt-relative, length-normalized response surprisal and prompt pass rate. ExPPO combines bounded shaping with shared normalization to preserve verifier polarity and approximately maintain each prompt group's total absolute sequence-advantage mass. Our analysis characterizes response-level credit allocation alongside sampled mode updates, deriving local conditions for gains in entropy and correct-mode discovery. Experiments show improved in-domain and out-of-domain reasoning coverage, higher aggregate response accuracy, and strong coverage at large sampling budgets. A controlled multi-answer evaluation further demonstrates increased correct-mode yield and gains in diversity among verified-correct responses. Code is available at https://github.com/jinhangzhan/ExPPO

</details>


### [95] [Beyond the Parameter Monolith: Reconstructive Memories, Executable Skills, and Residual Assembly for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04012)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文探究FEM-ASM架构，将语言模型拆分为上下文计算、持久存储与精确执行的分离组织，并用受控实验和负面结果加以评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在不再通过单一共享参数系统更新所有能力，而是探索存储、执行与神经协调相分离的组织方式。

Method: 构建受有限元方法启发的FEM-ASM：独立文档状态与确定性可执行技能向共享语言模型状态提出带类型的提案，用显式残差算子协调共同接口节点上的提案；包含无注意力Multi-Mesh原型、版本化存储（52,809个重构记忆元素、约17亿浮点值预算）、支持感知词法索引，以及可执行算术中的位置化结果观测与输出替换。

Result: Multi-Mesh可学习因果语言建模但未达到有竞争力的通用能力；存储重构不完整，令牌准确率约75%；位置化结果观测显著优于重复全局结果向量，输出替换会改变模型偏好答案；有界附着演示测量了使选定证据可用的效果。

Conclusion: 结果支持存储、执行与神经协调的分离，同时指出仅问题检索、无限制答案生成与端到端效率方面尚未解决的局限。

Abstract: Language-model systems can separate contextual computation, persistent storage, and exact execution instead of updating all capabilities through one shared parameter system. We investigate FEM-ASM, a finite-element-method-inspired organization in which independently constructed document states and deterministic executable skills contribute typed proposals to a shared language-model state. An explicit residual operator reconciles proposals attached to common interface nodes. We evaluate this organization through controlled experiments and negative results rather than claiming a physical finite-element formulation of language. An attention-free Multi-Mesh prototype learns causal language modeling but does not establish competitive general capability. A versioned store contains 52,809 reconstructive memory elements near a 1.7-billion-floating-value budget; reconstruction is incomplete, with approximately 75\% token accuracy. Support-aware lexical indices make these elements addressable under provenance-controlled query construction. For executable arithmetic, positional result observations substantially improve neural rendering relative to a repeated global result vector, and output substitutions change the model's preferred answer. A bounded attachment demonstration further measures the effect of making selected evidence available, without establishing the utility of loading an entire multi-billion-value store. The results support a separation of storage, execution, and neural coordination, while identifying unresolved limitations in question-only retrieval, unrestricted answer generation, and end-to-end efficiency.

</details>


### [96] [A Quantitative Analysis of Graph Representation Strategies for Cyber Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04019)
*Ali Melih Kanca,Ilker Turker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对2019—2026年间37项基于图的网络攻击检测研究进行定量分析，统计其表示策略在各应用领域的分布。结果显示自动表示学习占73.0%，手工表示占27.0%，且应用领域与表示策略之间存在显著关联（p=0.008，Cramer's V=0.540）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的网络攻击检测研究在构建与表示策略上差异较大，且分布在不同网络安全应用领域，这种多样性促使作者需要定量考察表示策略在各领域间的分布情况。

Method: 对2019至2026年间发表的37项原创研究进行编码，编码维度包括发表年份、应用领域、图表示类型、特征提取策略、学习范式、算法与数据集；随后采用频率分析、交叉列表及统计关联检验进行分析。

Result: 自动表示学习是最常用的策略，占73.0%，手工表示策略占27.0%。Fisher-Freeman-Halton精确检验显示，应用领域与表示策略之间存在统计显著关联（精确p=0.008；Cramer's V=0.540）。

Conclusion: 在所分析的文献中，自动表示学习占主导地位，且表示策略的分布在不同网络安全应用领域之间存在差异。

Abstract: Graph based cyber attack detection studies employ various graph construction and representation strategies across different cybersecurity application domains. This diversity motivates a quantitative examination of how representation strategies are distributed across these application domains. This study presents a quantitative analysis of 37 original studies published between 2019 and 2026. Each study was coded according to publication year, application domain, graph representation type, feature extraction strategy, learning paradigm, algorithm, and dataset. Frequency analysis, cross tabulation, and statistical association tests were applied. Automated representation learning was the most frequently employed strategy, accounting for 73.0% of the studies, while handcrafted representation strategies accounted for 27.0%. The Fisher Freeman Halton exact test revealed a statistically significant association between application domain and representation strategy (exact p = 0.008; Cramer's V = 0.540). The findings indicate that automated representation learning predominates within the analyzed corpus and that the distribution of representation strategies differs across cybersecurity application domains.

</details>


### [97] [The Reported Engagement with AI Level (REAL) Rating: A Framework for Disclosing Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04021)
*Imène Goumiri,Mayleen Cortez-Rodriguez,Eric Bell,Amanda Muyskens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出REAL Rating框架，用六层级（0-5级）量表披露AI在产品创作与用户体验中的参与程度，适用于文本、图像、音频、视频和软件产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已广泛融入数字媒体与生产流程，但现有溯源标准和披露方法依赖二元分类（完全人工创作vs.AI生成）或缺乏用户可理解性的技术水印，无法区分AI不同使用方式带来的不同风险与结果。

Method: 提出Reported Engagement with AI Level（REAL）Rating框架，建立六层级（Levels 0-5）量表，用于披露AI在产品创作和用户体验两方面的参与程度，并详细阐述其结构化方法论及在文本、图像、音频、视频、软件产品中的应用细则。

Result: 论文详述了REAL Rating系统的结构化方法及其在五类媒介产品（文本、图像、音频、视频、软件）中的具体应用规范。

Conclusion: REAL Rating框架通过细粒度、面向用户的AI参与度披露，弥补了现有二元分类和纯技术水印的不足，为不同AI使用场景下的风险区分提供了结构化解决方案。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has become increasingly incorporated into digital media and more generally into production workflows with which the public frequently interacts. Current provenance standards and disclosure methods frequently rely on binary categorizations, differentiating only between entirely human-authored and AI-generated content, or depend on technical watermarking that lacks user-facing clarity. However, there are very different risks and outcomes depending on the different uses of AI in the creation and consumption of end products. Therefore, we propose the Reported Engagement with AI Level (REAL) Rating framework which addresses these concerns by introducing a six-tier scale (Levels 0-5) that discloses the extent of AI involvement in both product creation and user experience. This paper details the structural methodology of the REAL Rating system and the specifics of its application across text, image, audio, video, and software products.

</details>


### [98] [Agent Policy-Value Audit: Separating Transition Composition from Event Selection in Financial LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04040)
*Mingyang,Chen,Yida,Xu,Huiwen,Chen,Yiming Lu,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体策略-价值审计"方法，通过固定各有序动作变更类型的观测次数并在合格事件间随机重分配，将部署价值分解为组合基准与选择价值，从而分离金融LLM智能体的事件选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融LLM智能体评估多采用端到端收益与基线配对差异检验，仅衡量部署是否改变实现绩效，无法分离事件选择技能。由于事件池本身呈上行漂移，智能体即便随机选择事件，只要频繁从空仓转为多头，也能获得正的配对收益，导致被动暴露被误判为选择能力。

Method: 提出Agent Policy-Value Audit：固定观测到的每种有序动作变更类型的数量，将其在合格事件间随机重分配，重分配所得平均收益即为组合基准；观测部署价值与组合基准之差为选择价值。基于真实财报事件收益构建半合成基准进行验证，并回顾性应用于44家美国消费类企业的723个财报事件。

Result: 在半合成基准中，以零为中心的配对检验在11.6%的无技能重复中错误地将被动暴露归因于选择技能，而转移匹配审计将该比率降至5.3%。回顾性应用中，智能体每事件总部署价值为+15.2 bps，分解为+25.8 bps的组合基准与-10.6 bps的选择价值，智能体未能显著优于匹配随机分配。

Conclusion: 金融智能体评估应分别报告部署价值与事件选择价值，转移匹配审计能有效剔除被动暴露带来的虚假技能归因，避免高估智能体的选择能力。

Abstract: Financial LLM agents are often evaluated by comparing their end-to-end returns with those of a baseline and testing the paired difference against zero. This measures whether deploying the agent changes realized performance, but it does not isolate event-selection skill. An agent that frequently changes positions from flat to long can earn a positive paired return from an upward-drifting event pool even when it selects events at random. We propose the Agent Policy-Value Audit, which holds fixed the observed count of each ordered action-change type and randomly reassigns them across eligible events. The average payoff from these reassignments is the composition benchmark; the difference between observed deployment value and this benchmark is selection value. In semi-synthetic benchmarks based on real earnings-event returns, a zero-centered paired test falsely attributes passive exposure to selection skill in $11.6\%$ of no-skill replications, while the transition-matched audit reduces this rate to $5.3\%$. Applied retrospectively to 723 earnings events at 44 U.S. consumer-facing firms, the audit decomposes the agent's gross deployment value of $+15.2$ bps/event into a $+25.8$ composition benchmark and a $-10.6$ selection value. The agent does not detectably outperform matched random assignments. Financial-agent evaluations should report deployment value separately from event-selection value.

</details>


### [99] [The Cost of a Hop: Benchmarking NLIP and A2A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04053)
*Ranjan Sinha,Anindita Das,Ashika Anand Babu,Hari Palleti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对NLIP与A2A两种LLM智能体协议进行受控性能对比，将延迟分解为消息创建、连接与发送三阶段，跨三种硬件环境测量，并给出按工作负载选型的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自主智能体需要标准化协议实现跨系统互操作，目前已出现A2A、MCP、ACP、ANP、NLIP等多种协议。然而NLIP尚未出现在任何受控性能研究中，也无人测量智能体协议的延迟究竟花在哪个环节。本文旨在填补这一空白，对NLIP与A2A进行实证比较并定位延迟瓶颈。

Method: 在三种独立硬件环境中，对NLIP与A2A协议进行经验对比，将端到端延迟分解为消息创建、连接建立和消息发送三个阶段分别测量。同时对A2A SDK启用连接缓存以测试其最优表现，并与更优化的Python-A2A实现进行对照。

Result: 在轻量级协调场景下，NLIP在两种环境中比基线A2A SDK快8.4–9.6倍，第三种环境约快4倍，优势方向一致但幅度依赖硬件。优势具有阶段特异性：在LLM推理占主导的端到端流水线中，两协议接近持平；差异几乎全部来自连接建立阶段。启用连接缓存后，A2A与NLIP的差距依硬件缩小，从某机器的2.75倍到更快硬件上的接近持平，大规模下缓存优化的A2A-SDK可与NLIP匹敌。相比更优化的Python-A2A，NLIP在同一阶段领先约4倍。

Conclusion: 协议性能优势具有阶段特异性和硬件依赖性：NLIP的优势主要来自连接建立而非发送阶段，在LLM推理主导的场景中优势消失。作者以实测条件的形式报告结果，未对残余的发送阶段开销给出单一因果解释，并最终提供了依据工作负载特征进行协议选型的指南。

Abstract: Autonomous agents built on Large Language Models (LLMs) need standardized protocols to interoperate across systems. Several now exist (A2A, MCP, ACP, ANP, NLIP), but the Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP) has not appeared in any controlled performance study, and no work has measured where an agent protocol's latency is spent. We compare NLIP and the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) protocol empirically, decomposing latency into message creation, connection, and send phases across three independent hardware environments. For lightweight coordination, NLIP is 8.4-9.6x faster than the baseline A2A SDK implementation on two environments and about 4x on a third; the direction of the advantage is consistent, its magnitude depends on the hardware. The advantage is stage-specific: for the end-to-end pipeline, where LLM inference dominates, the protocols are near parity. The difference comes almost entirely from connection setup. To test A2A at its best, we also ran A2A SDK with connection caching enabled; caching narrows its gap with NLIP by a hardware-dependent amount, from 2.75x on one machine to near-parity on faster hardware, where at scale a cache-optimized A2A-SDK matches NLIP. We report these as measured conditions without a single causal account of the residual send-phase cost. Against the more optimized Python-A2A, NLIP leads by about 4x on the same stage. We close with a protocol-selection guide keyed to workload characteristics.

</details>


### [100] [Discrete Diffusion for Large Graph Generation via Structural Candidate Restriction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04056)
*Yassin Mohamadi,Zeno Geradts,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离散图扩散模型，通过仅在结构相关的节点对（观测边及其楔形非边）上训练，并设计三分类吸收式前向过程和度感知、带下界的余弦噪声调度，将训练复杂度降至亚二次，从而生成结构统计逼真的大规模稀疏图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图规模大，但真实样本有限或访问敏感，因此需要大规模合成图。现有扩散式生成器建模能力强，却多为二次复杂度，仅支持约3k节点的小网络；非二次方法又受限于记忆问题和可扩展性与生成质量之间的权衡。作者希望在不记忆训练数据的前提下，生成结构统计（度分布、聚类系数、路径长度）忠实反映真实稀疏图的大规模图。

Method: 提出离散图扩散模型：训练仅限制在结构上有动机的节点对子集——观测边及其楔形非边，使训练复杂度低于节点数的二次。设计三分类吸收式前向过程，由“度感知、带下界”的余弦噪声调度控制；与对所有节点对同等加噪的结构盲调度不同，该调度根据每个节点的度自适应，且从不会完全抹除图结构，使前向与反向轨迹中的噪声图在每一步都保持信息量。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，在大规模图生成任务中，该模型在结构保真度上相较于现有离散扩散基线，始终位居最佳方法之列。

Conclusion: 通过结构相关的节点对采样和度感知的噪声调度，该离散扩散模型在保持信息量的同时将复杂度降至亚二次，能够生成结构统计忠实于真实稀疏图的大规模图，并在结构保真度上达到领先水平。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic graphs at scale is vital when the graphs of interest are large and real-world samples are limited or access-sensitive. Diffusion-based generators have recently driven much of the progress, offering high modeling capacity, but most such methods have quadratic computational complexity and are hence restricted to small-scale networks, currently up to 3k nodes. Existing non-quadratic methods remain limited by memorization issues and a trade-off between scalability and generation quality. Our goal is to generate large graphs whose structural statistics --- e.g., degree distribution, clustering, and path length --- faithfully reflect those of real-world sparse graphs, without resorting to memorizing the training data. We introduce a discrete graph diffusion model that restricts training to a structurally motivated subset of node pairs --- observed edges and their wedge non-edges --- reducing training complexity below quadratic in the number of nodes. To keep the noisy graph informative throughout both the forward and reverse trajectories, we design a three-class absorbing forward process governed by a \emph{degree-aware, floored} cosine noise schedule: unlike a structure-blind schedule that adds noise to every pair identically, ours adapts to each node's degree and never fully erases the graph's structure, keeping the noisy graph informative at every step. Experiments across diverse datasets show that our model consistently ranks among the top methods for structural fidelity against existing discrete diffusion baselines in large graph generation.

</details>


### [101] [Reinforcement Learning with Comparative Evidence for Social Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04072)
*Keane Ong,Yuriel Ryan,Sabri Boughorbel,Vladimir Necula,Jack Wei Lun Shi,Rui Mao,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Adriel Kuek,Nancy F. Chen,Erik Cambria,Gianmarco Mengaldo,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLCE（基于对比证据的强化学习），在不使用标注数据构建奖励的前提下，从无标注数据中学习社会理解能力，在情感、语用、交际意图和偏好四类基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会智能AI的发展高度依赖人工标注数据，限制了模型可习得的社会理解的规模与广度。从无标注数据中获取训练信号是突破这一依赖的路径，但社会性预测缺乏数学和编程领域那样的验证标准答案（oracle）。此外，情感、意图、偏好、语用含义等核心社会目标本身具有模糊性，同一行为可能支持多种合理解释，难以验证哪一种解释最有依据。

Method: 提出RLCE（Reinforcement Learning with Comparative Evidence）。给定一组 rollout 中产生的不同答案，RLCE 构造"证据测试"来识别可观察证据，用以支持某一答案优于另一答案；随后针对输入样本验证这些测试，并聚合测试结果以确定最有依据的解释。随着策略生成新答案，测试会被重新生成，从而与策略共同演化。整个过程不使用真实标注来构造奖励。

Result: 在覆盖情感、语用、交际意图和偏好的四个基准上，RLCE 在七种不使用真实标注作为奖励的方法中表现最强，这些对比方法包括共识法、策略LLM评判验证、多模态协同演化和基于评分细则（rubric）的奖励。相较最强基线，提升幅度最高达 +18.93 分。分析进一步表明：RLCE 在正确与错误预测之间的奖励差异占比高于评分细则方法，能够推翻策略自身产生的错误偏好，并从成对测试构造、组合式测试聚合和在线策略测试演化中获益。

Conclusion: RLCE 证明了无需真实标注即可从无标注数据中学习社会理解，通过对比证据构造与策略共同演化的验证测试，有效应对社会预测的模糊性与缺乏验证oracle的难题，在多个社会理解维度上超越现有无标注奖励方法。

Abstract: Developing socially intelligent AI remains heavily dependent on human-annotated data, limiting the scale and breadth of social understanding models can acquire. Methods that derive training signals from unlabeled data offer a path beyond this dependence, but social predictions lack the verification oracles available in mathematics and coding. Moreover, core social targets such as affect, intent, preference, and pragmatic meaning are often ambiguous. The same behavior can support multiple plausible interpretations, making it difficult to verify which is best supported. To address this challenge, we introduce Reinforcement Learning with Comparative Evidence (RLCE), a reinforcement learning method that learns social understanding from unlabeled training data without constructing rewards from ground-truth annotations.
